## Candidate evidence

- 101534291, 125918626, 22952891: repeated multi-pixel off-target localization;
  not planets around searched targets. First source near a Gaia ECL-classified
  neighbor, but Gaia/TESS periods disagree; do not imply matched ephemerides.
- 394015919, 467361658, 405415381, 302366928, 312812096, 317594826, 290653184,
  452629994: unpromoted based on the recorded extraction, variability, pixels,
  timing and/or prediction evidence. Consult individual assessments/reports.
- **301798878 remains an unresolved weak lead**, P~19.6185d. Covered QLP events
  and both Sector 44 core differences support dimming, but 5/48 block controls
  exceed observed and no decisive independent recovery exists. The global
  difference centroid lands elsewhere because of another residual; that alone
  is NOT an off-target veto. SIMBAD primary alias LP 410-81 / HIP 12642; nearby
  TIC 471011362 is explicitly a duplicate, not a separate resolved star.
- 290653184: 0/48 block exceedances do NOT establish a planet. Its refined
  expanded-training ephemeris fails Sector 61 despite expected formal S/N~11;
  Sector 72 is insufficient despite total S/N~6.2. Strong pixel dipole.
- New batch leads: 279569731 and 247528319 have clear off-target signals in two
  sectors each. 47961197 has weak QLP support and inconsistent residual images;
  17619251 has a broad detrending-sensitive signal unsupported by QLP, but its
  pixel difference is central, not an off-target veto.
- Retry lead 288269034 and exposure-companion priorities 51912266/97060271 are
  unpromoted: broad, detrending-sensitive features with weak/uneven QLP support.
  The latter two have central pixel dimming, not off-target vetoes. No heldout
  observations opened. See the retry assessment.
- Early batch-seven passes 428888308/459763099 already have saved discovery,
  QLP and pixel checks: both broad and detrending-sensitive, with extraction
  disagreement. Not promoted; no heldouts opened. Do not duplicate these checks.
- Batch-seven 88115513 also inspected: fairly stable localized dip, weak QLP
  support and structured pixel residuals. No clean off-target veto, no likely
  planet established. Sector 72 remains unopened.
- Batch-eight companion 150189995 inspected: noisy feature, model S/N 6.01,
  QLP weak/inconsistent and image residuals with adjacent negative pixels.
  Not promoted; all reserved sectors unopened. No resolved contaminant assigned.
- **399450592: matching ZTF neighbor eclipses strongly support contamination.** P~1.74285d, original
  depth 649 ppm, stable detrending, QLP Sector 24 support. Older sectors 18/52
  added before freezing; held-out 58 recovers at S/N 7.98 with precise-enough
  original timing diagnostic. 0/48 block exceedances is not validation. Pixels
  24/25 point toward faint TIC 399450579; 58 shifts in a different sky direction.
  No Gaia variability entry for either source. Source attribution unresolved.
  Physical fits (already-opened 18/24/25/52/58) are compatible with ~1.8–1.9 Earth
  radii under circular/on-target/stellar-prior assumptions; free geometry is
  degenerate and one limb-darkening trial hits a boundary. NOT a radius
  measurement or likelihood of planethood. Sector 78 remains unopened.
  Completed target/neighbor pixel injections: the neighbor hypothesis matches
  both discovery sectors much better; Sector 58 poorly matches either. This is
  conditional diagnostic evidence, not a source probability. See assessment.
- **235596068 likely eclipsing stellar system:** comparable deep secondary at
  phase 0.655, eight events, QLP confirmation in 52/53; image differences central.
  Reserved 74/79/80 unopened. See batch-thirteen assessment.
- **83968926 is another unresolved lead**, P~13.5275d, four consistent events
  and QLP Sector 18 support. Pixels ambiguous. 0/48 block exceedances does not
  establish a planet. Sector 24 adds no event leverage; frozen Sector 58 timing
  spread is 0.776d and fixed S/N -0.54. Exploratory shifts over that window reach
  only S/N 2.82; no recovery. Sectors 78/85 remain unopened. Consult the batch-five
  assessment and follow-up evidence before further work.
- QLP Sector 58 now also checked for 83968926 at the old frozen ephemeris:
  S/N -0.40; exploratory timing-window maximum 2.63. No recovery in either
  extraction. Script/data/results archived; no additional holdout opened.

- **80387959 promising unresolved signal:** 4.192-day dip, nine discovery events,
  QLP support, central difference images in 36/37. Added older Sector 10 before
  freeze/opening 63. Fixed-time 63 S/N 7.06, five events; timing spread 0.052585d
  narrowly exceeds preset half-duration threshold, so original conclusion stays
  inconclusive. 0/48 block exceedances is not validation. Pixel 63 offset ~0.57
  pixels; target-injection controls show 1/47 larger offsets, versus 38/47 and
  6/47 in 36/37. Neighbor controls terminal: neither 80387965 nor 80387961 fits all three sectors. No likely
  planet or novelty established. Follow-up, neighborhood, nulls complete.

## Sensitivity and method limits

- Earlier 38/40 recovery probes used one period/duration and top-three recovery.
- Broader 96 real-data injections: 55 recovered periods, 33 full-screen passes;
  exposure-model companion diagnostics retain 36, preserving all original passes.
  Not completeness. Some strong injections still fail heuristic cuts.
- Exposure model uses an integrated box, local linear baselines, continuous
  timing/duration and beta-inflated event errors. It is not a limb-darkened fit
  or calibrated significance. Both known-planet controls pass; synthetic odd/even
  eclipses remain detectable. Reports: `RECOVERY-AUDIT` and `EXPOSURE-MODEL-AUDIT`.
- `model_rescreen` supports explicit SPOC/QLP selection, validates collection and
  carries through missing-error flags. All 173 newer QLP successes received
  selection checks; no companion priority. Companions cover all eight batches and
  retry: no extra in batches five/six/seven; two extra in retry, one in eight.
  Original evidence retained.
- Residual masking recovers nine of 41 formerly missed injection periods, but
  zero new full-screen passes. Known L98-59 b/d recovered after masking c and b;
  GJ 357 has no residual pass. A frozen 20-target real residual pilot completes
  with no errors or priorities. Not completeness; alternate-global-best check
  can penalize secondary signals. See `RESIDUAL-AND-QLP-AUDIT-20261009.md`.
